# Upstream Adoption 0.13.4: Task Board, Tasks and Planning, Projects, Pages (1.42.0)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task by task.
> - Every row begins by verifying its premise at HEAD.
> - A row already closed at HEAD is a result to report, not a reason to build.
> - Read upstream with `git -C .references/craft-agents-oss show v0.13.4:<path>`. Never edit `.references/`.

**Goal:** Port the following from upstream craft-agents-oss v0.13.4 into Nessy Desktop, as fully as the owner's port-maximal rule allows:
- the Kanban board;
- durable Tasks;
- planning: Generate mode, the Conductor, acceptance grading with repair, and the plan dispatcher;
- Projects;
- Pages.

Everything is adapted to our three runtimes through the host seam and ships AVAILABLE: nothing is gated (owner, 2026-09-23). Craft's public share service is cut. Page publishing is deferred until the owner supplies the internal artifact resource (UA6.7).

**Architecture:**
- The host owns every durable object and decision: SessionManager, stores, `TaskRunner` behind `SessionConductorHost`, and the MACed approvals ledger.
- Pi, Claude and Codex run sessions and reach new host tools through the Pi harness descriptor, the Claude callback registry and the Codex host-callback bridge.
- Capability variance is DATA (tool projection, `runtime-switch-costs.ts`), never renderer branches.

**Tech Stack:** Bun 1.3.14, TypeScript, Electron ^39 + React renderer (jotai, `@dnd-kit/core`), zod, `js-yaml`, `bun:sqlite` (Bun-only subpath), i18n JSON (en, ru), vitepress docs-site, CDP harness in `scripts/cdp/`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-23-upstream-0-13-4-board-planning-pages-design.md`. Its section 2 explains what upstream calls planning.

**Born:** 2026-09-23.
- Owner decisions 1–4 plus the same-day addendum: "port to us AS MUCH AS POSSIBLE … Their frontend can be copied directly".
- Drops are allowed only for (a) a Craft external service, (b) a security posture with no safe adaptation, or (c) an existing Nessy feature that does it better, citing the file.
- This reverses the 2026-07-19 "do not adopt kanban/tasks".
- Synthesised from three drafts and six critic reviews (spec §20).
- Revised the same day for two later owner directives (Phase 0.1 items 5 and 6; spec §1 rows 5 and 6). Nothing is gated, so the switch rows are gone and every ported feature ships available (spec §6). Page publishing is DEFERRED to the owner's internal artifact resource rather than dropped (spec §3, UA6.7).
- Then corrected by three critic reviews of that revision (residue, safety, format). Their accepted findings are folded in, and the one declined design change is recorded in spec §20.

**Sequencing:**
- The plan starts after the `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-18-integrations-followup-1.41.0.md` close-out (F1.54–F1.59). It targets 1.42.0 on `feat/TCODE-000-1-42-0` (OQ-5).
- Phase 1 comes first, on the release branch.
- **Board lane:** Phases 2→3→4→5, on the release branch. Phase 5 dispatches on UA5.1, meaning the owner's answer or the default taken at the wave boundary.
- **Pages lane:** Phases 6→7→8→9, running in parallel with the board lane after Phase 1, on its own branch `feat/TCODE-000-1-42-0-pages`.
  - The branch is cut from the release branch once Phase 1 has landed. It works in its own worktree with its own `bun install --frozen-lockfile`. Never symlink `node_modules` into it (AGENTS.md's worktree trap), and assert provenance before trusting a number. `.references/` is gitignored and so absent from the worktree; read upstream from the main tree's `.references/craft-agents-oss`.
  - Before each unit's packaged sitting, the lane takes the release branch in with `git merge`, never a rebase, because its shas are pinned. The sitting then measures what the merge will produce.
  - The controller merges a whole UNIT into the release branch with `git merge --no-ff`, never a squash (AGENTS.md's squash-merge trap). It does so only when every row of the unit is ✅ and the unit's live cases PASS. The units are Phases 6+7 (library, tools and UI together), then Phase 8, then Phase 9.
  - UA6.7 (⏸, owner-blocked) is not needed for any unit to be complete. It lands as a unit of its own once the owner's resource arrives, and only after the 6+7 unit has merged.
  - The branch is kept, never deleted, while any tracker cell cites its shas.
- Phases 10 and 11 come last, on the release branch.
- **Release cuts.** Nothing is gated, so a phase ships only when it is user-complete: no visible entry leads to a withheld range or a missing handler, and every live case of its capabilities PASSES in the packaged sitting. The valid cuts are:
  - the end of Phase 1 (nothing it adds is a half-built surface, and UA1.12 proves it live);
  - on the board lane, the end of Phase 2 (the board over existing sessions), Phase 4 (tasks and planning together) and Phase 5. **The end of Phase 3 is not a cut:** it creates specs with subtasks, and nothing can run them until Phase 4;
  - on the pages lane, each merged unit.

  The board lane lands on the release branch, so 1.42.0 is cut only at one of its valid boundaries.
  - **Naming the board cut.** The controller names the board-lane cut boundary (end of Phase 2, 4 or 5) before dispatching the next board phase. Once it is named, no later board row is dispatched on the release branch until 1.42.0 is cut. Board phases after the boundary move to 1.43.0, like unmerged pages units.
  - **Unmerged pages units.** A pages unit not complete by the 1.42.0 cut is NOT merged. Its rows move to 1.43.0 (UA11.1) rather than shipping hidden.

## Global Constraints

- **Git:**
  - Commit only with `git commit -F <msgfile> -- <pathspec>`, and follow the new-file recipe in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-07-wave-9-brief.md`.
  - Never use `-m` with backticks, `--amend`, `stash`, or a bare `git add` followed by a commit. Never `checkout --`, `restore` or `reset` another lane's file.
  - Subject: `<type>(<scope>): UAx.y — <sentence>`. No `Co-Authored-By`.
  - No MR, tag, push or publish.
  - One commit per row, with one recorded exception (a deviation): a row that introduces i18n readers lands TWO commits, keys first and reader second. Its commit cell lists both shas joined by ` + `.
- **Zones:** read them from `git status` at dispatch, in the lane's own tree. §0.8 lists serialised files. The board lane and the pages lane never hold a §0.8 file at the same time, on either branch. The controller dispatches those rows one at a time across both lanes, so a unit merge never meets two lanes' fresh hunks in one file.
- **Verification budget:**
  - In the loop, run focused suites alone via `bun scripts/bun-test-lock.ts run --label <lane> -- bun test ./<path>`, with `./` on every path. **Read the FILE COUNT.** Also run the affected-package typecheck and `git diff --check`.
  - Never pipe a gate, never wrap it in `timeout`, and run long gates detached.
  - At the end of each LANE, run `typecheck:all` and `lint` detached, plus every UA test file of that lane, file by file. For the board lane that is after the last row of the named cut boundary. For the pages lane it is at the end of each unit, in its worktree.
  - After a unit's merge, run that unit's UA test files again, file by file, on the release branch. The test lock is machine-wide (`/tmp`), so it serialises both worktrees.
  - Whole-repo gates run again only in UA11.7.
- **i18n:**
  - Keys go into `en.json` and `ru.json`, flat, sorted, written as characters.
  - A row adds exactly the keys its reader uses: keys commit first, reader commit second.
  - There are no locale-only rows. Never read `lint:i18n:reachable` between the two commits.
  - The ported `check-i18n-coverage` gate (UA1.11) derives the key list from the row's files.
- **Settings keys:** no row in this plan adds a StoredConfig key.
  - The only one was the withdrawn `experimentalSwitches`. Board appearance and project colour treatment are per-viewer localStorage, as upstream.
  - A row that finds it needs a key is a new row with its reason. It declares the key in `StoredConfigKnownFields` (`packages/shared/src/config/stored-config-load.ts`) per the DA1.72 docblock in `validators.ts`, and lands reader, envelope and guard fixture in ONE commit.
- **Channels:**
  - Classify every new channel in `packages/shared/src/protocol/routing.ts`, and map it in `apps/electron/src/transport/channel-map.ts`.
  - Regenerate `bun run scripts/ipc-inventory.ts` in the same row.
  - A NEW `packages/server-core/src/handlers/rpc/<module>.ts` exports `HANDLED_CHANNELS`. In the same commit:
    - add a row to `rpc/__tests__/rpc-handled-channels-agreement.test.ts`;
    - add the module to `apps/electron/src/main/handlers/__tests__/registration.test.ts` `getExpectedChannels`;
    - add it to `registration-profiles.test.ts`.
  - Every handler calls the lifted `assertCallerWorkspace` (UA1.9). No handler carries a feature gate or a `feature-disabled` refusal (spec §6).
- **Session tools:** each new or bridged tool updates all of the following in ONE commit:
  - `tool-defs.ts`: the definition and both host-callback sets. There is no `SessionToolFilterOptions` field and no `isOfferedByHostPorts` arm, because nothing is gated. The tool is offered wherever the runtime hosts session tools; on codex that means the spawn has the host-callback bridge (spec §5);
  - `packages/session-tools-core/src/host-callback-bridge.ts` `HOST_CALLBACK_BRIDGE_ROUTES`;
  - `packages/session-mcp-server/src/host-callback-bridge-client.ts`;
  - `packages/shared/src/agent/backend/codex/session-callback-bridge.ts`;
  - `harness-tool-descriptor.ts` (explicit `external` + `sessionBinding`, traced on `set_session_status`);
  - `tool-surface.json`: the tool's row.
    - Its `availabilityCondition` inherits the session-tools collection profile unless the row needs an override.
    - An override follows the wording of the existing bridged override: "externalStandaloneOnly excludes it as host-callback dependent unless the bridge carries HOST_CALLBACK_BRIDGE_ROUTES.<route>".
    - It never names a feature or switch condition;
  - `packages/pi-agent-server/src/harness/source-state-contract.ts` (`liveJsonSchemaDigests`) and `live-manifest-compositions.ts`;
  - the comment above the `claude-agent.ts` `availableToolNames` call, whose measured name counts move. The call needs no option: `getSessionToolNames()` is the Claude projection of the registry;
  - `apps/electron/src/renderer/components/app-shell/input/runtime-switch-costs.ts`;
  - `scheduling-projection.test.ts`, `external-standalone.test.ts`, both `runtime-capability-drift.test.ts`, `runtime-switch-costs-surface-counts.test.ts`;
  - then run `cd packages/pi-agent-server && bun run check:surface`.
- **Copying:**
  - Rebrand `Craft`→`Nessy`, `@craft-agent`→`@nessy-agent`, `CRAFT_`→`NESSY_`, `craft-pages/v1`→`nessy-pages/v1`, `craft-*` localStorage keys→`nessy-*`.
  - The header records `v0.13.4:<path>`. Adapted prompt text records its source and licence.
  - A **staged copy** records each withheld upstream range in its header as `withheld: v0.13.4 L<a>–L<b> → UA<x>`. The owning row removes the line, and no visible entry of a shipped cut may lead into a withheld range.
  - Shipped docs are REWRITTEN and land with their code.
- **The manifest is authoritative for file→row.** Re-derive a row's Files list from it before dispatch.
- **Playground:** extend canonical entries with knobs, and sweep all states in dark theme.
- **Tests:**
  - Every invariant gets at least one positive and one negative test, named in the row.
  - A row with no explicit Done means is done when three things hold:
    - every test arm it names passes alone with the expected FILE COUNT;
    - its affected-package typecheck is green;
    - every file in its Files list (re-derived from the manifest) is committed.
- **Live cases:**
  - Sitting cases are `scripts/cdp/cases/rs-NNN.ts`. Each one's `meta.row` names the UA row, and each is registered in `scripts/cdp/run.ts` `SITTING`.
  - **RS ids are allocated by the controller on the release branch at dispatch.** The controller writes the row's "case owed (UAx.y)" checklist row in `docs/testing/cdp-live-checklist.md` in a pin commit. The pages lane takes that commit in with its next merge before authoring the case. `rs-108.ts` exists on 2026-09-23.
  - Nothing is gated, so a case carries no switch precondition.
  - **Every capability that ships has its cases PASS in the packaged sitting before the release cut.** A case that cannot pass holds its phase or unit out of the release (Sequencing, UA11.1).
  - Author and wire the cases offline in-phase. Drive them in the lane's packaged sitting (the `env -u` launcher, `bypassPermissions` unless the card is the subject, dark theme), and again in UA11.5.
- **No real credentials.**

## Review Focus

1. The frame CSP: first bytes, doctype-first, both render sites, no localhost. Plus the `event.source` check, activation with frame focus, and a thumbnail window that denies window-open and navigation.
2. No raw path reaches `createSession` (project folder, `spec.cwd`, tool, Generate, editor, Conductor dispatch).
3. No `allow-all`. The lifetime ceiling applies to Conductor children.
4. The completion seam: idle arm only, turn-scoped final text, error flag, settlement gate, queue mode, three send arms.
5. Codex reach is six artefacts, plus the Pi descriptor and the three surface pins.
6. The ledger is the only grant and approval authority, including confirmed spec digests and project-context digests. `runApprovedScript` is the only script entry and runs the verified bytes. The script env is an allowlist.
7. New setters go through `updateSessionMetadata` and the partition notify. Create fields go through `store-v2`.
8. Name collisions (spec §22 item 8).
9. Nothing is gated.
   - There is no switch key, env toggle, `feature-disabled` refusal, hidden entry or offer flag. Each surface's safety is the control spec §6 names.
   - The existing managed policies keep applying and none is added: `isLocalAutomationManagedDisabled` (through its three arms), `isManagedAllHooksDisabled`, the managed permission mode, and managed deny.
   - `isManagedAllHooksDisabled` covers runtime hooks, not the host `AutomationSystem`'s script actions.
10. i18n keys ride with their reader. Playground `categoryOrder`.
11. The release cut: a phase or unit ships only when user-complete with its live cases PASSING. A pages unit reaches the release branch whole, by a `--no-ff` merge, or not at all. Board phases after the named boundary wait (UA11.1).
12. The Craft share-service guard forbids Craft hosts and share-service knobs, never sharing as such (UA6.6).
13. Staged copies: no visible entry leads to a withheld range. This covers tile controls, TaskEditor tabs, `PagesHome`, `PageView` and `PageFrame`, and the page tools' `refresh`.

**Readers of this file:**
- `python3 scripts/sort-plan-tracker.py <this file> --check`: rows form one family, `UA<phase>.<n>`, sorted numerically.
- `--shas` (`lint:plan-shas`): commit cells hold `—` or real shas. In-cell pipes are written as "or".
- `scripts/plan-status.py` `VERDICT_SOURCE_PLANS` gets this plan in UA1.1.
- `build-parity-report.py` and `regenerate-deviation-register.py` read this file's full text; they are regenerated at close-out.
- `scripts/cdp/harness.test.ts` `PLANS` gets this plan in UA1.1.
- `check-doc-anchors.ts`: pages are named in words.

**Deviation recorded:** the controller pins rows (glyph + sha) in `docs(plan): <lane> pinned — …` commits (Dev-20 precedent). Lanes do not edit this file.
- The controller pins board-lane rows as they land.
- It pins a pages-lane row's commit cell only in the pin commit that follows its unit's `--no-ff` merge. Until then the row reads `🚧` with the note `on feat/TCODE-000-1-42-0-pages`.
- A sha that was never merged is never written into this file, so `lint:plan-shas` cannot meet an unreachable commit.
- The pages branch receives this file when it takes the release branch in.

## Phase 0: Read-me (prose only)

**0.1 Owner decisions (2026-09-23).**
1. Adopt the board; cover planning.
2. Fit our runtimes and host.
3. Pages behind a switch, OFF; sharing cut. **Reversed in part the same day by 5 and 6.**
4. Projects, script action and release are undecided (spec §21 has the defaults). Decision 5 settles the script action (OQ-4).
5. **Later the same day, reversing 3's switch:** «нет, закрывать ничего не нужно, делаем сразу как доступные фичи» ("no, nothing needs to be gated; we ship them straight away as available features").
   - There are no switches.
   - Every ported feature ships AVAILABLE by default: the board, tasks, planning, projects, Pages (core, UI, thumbnails), the script action, scheduled refresh, and page actions with grants.
   - OQ-3 is DECIDED "no switches".
   - The safety each switch stood for comes from the surface's own controls (spec §6). Existing managed policies keep applying, and none is added.
6. **The same day, on sharing:** «для артефактов я попозже дам наш внутренний скил/ресурс и подключим» ("for artifacts I will provide our internal skill/resource later, and we will connect it"). Craft's public share SERVICE stays dropped under reason (a). The page publishing FEATURE is DEFERRED until that resource arrives (UA6.7 ⏸, OQ-18). **Resolved later the same day:** the resource is the internal artifact service `https://t-artifacts.t-tech.team` (owner: «по артефактам да, использовать то что выше дал»), used through ONE host publisher for all three runtimes (UA1.16–UA1.23) and for Pages (UA6.7); the `artifact-publish` skill is not shipped. Chat sharing stays research only (spec §24).

The addendum makes PORT the default. Spec §3 is the complete drop and deferral list, and the manifest below is authoritative. A rewrite or drop that is not listed there needs a new row with its reason.

**0.2 Planning.** Generate, the DAG format, the Conductor, grading and repair, run controls, the plan dispatcher, and the Planner playground (spec §2). Upstream tasks are never scheduled; a user starts them.

**0.3 Pages.** Agent-authored, self-contained HTML in `{workspace}/pages/<slug>/`, shown in a sandboxed iframe. Each page has a KV and time-series store and six tools. Scheduled refresh is inert until a human approves its digest. Source actions run under grants. All of it is available. Craft's share service is CUT; publishing is DEFERRED to the owner's internal artifact resource (UA6.7).

**0.4 Our project is a folder handle** (`project-handle-service.ts`, `project-identity.ts`). Option (a′) keeps it under an upstream-shaped entity.

**0.5 Security findings carried into rows:**
- srcdoc inherits `connect-src https: wss:` and loopback (UA7.1);
- the thumbnail host, including window-open and navigation (UA7.4);
- Claude cloud tools (UA1.5);
- `allow-all` children (UA4.2);
- the `in_progress` example (UA1.2);
- codex `explore` (UA1.8);
- the `CRAFT_` env prefix (UA8.1);
- grants in `page.json` (UA1.10, UA9.2);
- script grants not pinned, and the swap window between check and spawn (UA8.1, UA9.2);
- the agent task escalation path (UA3.5, UA4.4);
- the Craft OAuth redirect in `sources.md` (UA6.5, dropped);
- project context from an agent-writable file (UA1.10, UA5.5);
- grant requests with no activation (UA9.3);
- the ledger key recoverable from `credentials.enc` (UA1.10).

**0.6 Refuted premises:**
- "Awaiting `sendMessage` serialises `max_parallel`": refuted, because upstream awaits too and dispatch is fire-and-forget.
- "Confirmation cards on all three runtimes": true only for `archive_session`.
- `spawn_session`, `set_session_status` and `set_session_labels` are not on codex today.
- The board has no column reorder.
- `in-progress` is not a default status.
- The `StoredConfigSchema` name is live.

**0.7 Findings from the critics, each owned by a row:**
- the completion seam must be turn-scoped and idle-arm only (UA1.4);
- node settlement (UA4.1, UA4.8);
- the Generate draft is not hidden and uses `dontAsk` (UA4.5);
- queue mode (UA4.1);
- `store-v2` create path (UA3.1);
- the `updateSessionMetadata` Pick (UA2.1);
- the private `assertCallerWorkspace` (UA1.9);
- the ASCII slug (UA3.4);
- the attach connection lock (UA3.4);
- resume from snapshot (UA4.3);
- the env allowlist (UA8.1);
- the ledger (UA1.10);
- the `index.css` token (UA2.3);
- `App.tsx` `handoffEventTypes` (UA2.1).

## Phase 1: Foundations and posture (`UA1.1`–`UA1.27`)

Nothing here is a half-built surface. Each user-visible piece — the closure guard, codex status and labels, and the label UI — is complete within its own row. UA1.12 proves them live. So the end of Phase 1 is a valid release cut.

**UA1.1 `[governance]` Governance wiring (XS).**
- **Files:**
  - `docs/README.md`: index this plan, the spec, and the 1.41.0 plan;
  - `scripts/cdp/harness.test.ts` `PLANS`;
  - `scripts/docs-integrity.test.ts` `ACTIVE`: repoint it, or record why not;
  - `scripts/plan-status.py` `VERDICT_SOURCE_PLANS`: this plan and the 1.41.0 plan, with a reason.
- **Tests:** harness positive: a fake sitting case whose `meta.row` is `UA2.7` resolves. Negative: a nonexistent `UA` id fails.
- **Done means:** all four readers know the plan.

**UA1.2 `[safety]` `[product]` Closure guard and truthful status text (S; OQ-6).**
- **Premise:** grep prompts, `statuses.md`, `automations.md`, docs-site and bundled automations for any agent-set `done`.
- **Files:**
  - `session-tools-core/src/handlers/set-session-status.ts`;
  - `context.ts` (`ResolvedStatusResult.category`);
  - `SessionManager.ts` (`resolveStatusFn`);
  - `tool-defs.ts`: the description carries the closure rule and real ids, and the `SetSessionStatusSchema` example is fixed;
  - `system.ts`: the self-closing steps end at `needs-review`;
  - `apps/electron/resources/docs/statuses.md`.
- **Tests:** `set-session-status.test.ts` (copy+adapt) and `status-examples-exist.test.ts`, which is scoped to `TOOL_DESCRIPTIONS.set_session_status` and the Setting status section.
  - Positive: `needs-review` is set.
  - Negative:
    - `done` and `cancelled` are refused, including when named by label;
    - neither scoped text names an id absent from the default config;
    - the codex projection's description equals the Pi and Claude description.

**UA1.16 `[host]` `[shared]` t-artifacts client, normaliser and registry (M; OQ-18, OQ-22 (f)).**
- **Premise (read 2026-09-23):** `loyalty-personalization/t-artifacts` at `14e402e` (`release-1.0.13`, npm `0.5.2`).
  - Create needs `Authorization: Bearer <dp token>` (`CreateHandler`, `internal/app/app/api/artifact_controller.go:104-109`); no edit token is issued (`skilldata/core.md` (lines 35–36), byte-identical to live `GET /skills/core`).
  - `PUT`, `PATCH` and `DELETE /api/artifacts/{id}` authorize the owner's dp identity or a legacy edit token (unclaimed pre-2026-09-02 artifacts only; `authorize`, `:446-466`). `GET /api/artifacts/mine` lists the caller's artifacts.
  - `PUT` and `PATCH` both accept `retention_days`; omitting it keeps the current `expires_at` (`internal/artifact/postgres_store.go` `Update`): an update does NOT extend the expiry.
  - Retention: default 7 days, 0 = unlimited, max 36500 (`internal/artifact/retention.go:8-9`); expired → 404, not renewable. `content` is capped at 16 MiB (`internal/artifact/artifact.go:38`).
  - HTML is wrapped at write time (`internal/htmlwrap/htmlwrap.go:48-81`): a full document nests, and title and emoji are written RAW into `<title>` and the favicon SVG; `ExtractTitle` lifts the first bare `<title>` anywhere (`:23-37`).
  - The stored source is downloadable by id with no auth (`GET /api/artifacts/{id}/content`, `:380-405`).
  - The landing page's "edit token shown once" and the service repo's `CLAUDE.md` (line 23) are stale.
  - Delegated JWTs exist since `022fead` (2026-09-23): RS256, `act.sub`, `aud` = the deployment's `SPIRIT_SYSTEM` (default `t-artifacts`, `internal/app/app/config.go:61`; production value UNVERIFIED).
- **Token custody (a stated product posture, shown on the docs page, not an implementation detail):**
  - v1 sends the opaque `dp auth print-token` value, a platform-wide credential, to a third-party service, which resolves it through devplatform `POST /api/v2/token-identity` (`internal/identity/spirit.go:135`).
  - Move to a delegated token as soon as dp can mint one (OQ-22 (f)).
  - Never send the Nestor JWT or anything read from `~/.nessy/dp_auth_creds.json`; never read `ARTIFACT_AUTH_TOKEN` / `T_ARTIFACTS_TOKEN` from the environment.
- **Files:**
  - create `packages/shared/src/artifacts/t-artifacts-client.ts` + test:
    - `createArtifact`, `updateArtifact`, `listOwnedArtifacts`, `patchArtifact` (`title`, `retention_days`), `deleteArtifact`;
    - the origin is a module constant `https://t-artifacts.t-tech.team`, with no env, setting or model override in v1; managed `deniedDomains` / `allowManagedDomainsOnly` are honoured before any request (kind `policy`);
    - the token comes from `printToken(<resolved dp path>, { timeoutMs })` (`packages/shared/src/auth/dp/dp-api.ts:109`) on every request and is never cached. Mapping: `undefined` → `needs-login`; `DpAuthServiceError` Timeout/Cancelled → `network`; `DpSpawnFailedError` / ExecFailed → `dp-unavailable`;
    - `fetch` with `redirect: 'error'`, so the Authorization header never follows a 3xx; 30 s budget; no `dp auth login`, no browser, no retry after login;
    - refuses to send (kind `network`, stated reason) when `BUN_CONFIG_VERBOSE_FETCH` is set or `NODE_DEBUG` names `fetch`, `undici` or `http`; logs carry status code and kind only, never headers or response bodies;
  - create `packages/shared/src/artifacts/normalize.ts` + test:
    - `.html`, `.htm` and `.md` only; ≤ 16 MiB after normalisation;
    - a full document becomes the body content plus the lifted `<title>` and head `<style>`/`<link>`/`<script>`; the lifted `<title>` is removed from the content, so the host-escaped `title` field is the one the service writes;
    - `title` is HTML-escaped by the host; `emoji` is restricted to 1–2 emoji code points (no `<`, `'`, `"`), because the service injects both raw;
    - v1 content policy: `<input type=password>` and `<form>` are refused; inline scripts and event-handler attributes are counted, and every external `src`/`href`/`url()` host is collected (both feed the card);
  - create `packages/shared/src/artifacts/registry.ts` + test:
    - `<workspaceRoot>/artifacts/registry.json` through `atomicWriteDurableFileSync` (`packages/shared/src/utils/durable-fs.ts:116`), mode 0600;
    - entries `{ id, url, title, createdAt, updatedAt, expiresAt, retentionDays, sourceSessionId, sourcePath, contentDigest, pendingDelete? }`;
    - the service stays the source of truth for the list; the registry maps artifacts to sessions and holds pending deletions;
    - its path pattern is added to `SAFE_MODE_CONFIG.deniedPaths` (`packages/shared/src/agent/mode-types.ts:623`) AND to the byte-identical `deniedPaths` in `apps/electron/resources/permissions/default.json` (`mode-types.ts:567-573`: a pattern added to only one of the two protects only half the installs);
  - fetch goes through the host network runtime: the global dispatcher in Electron main (`apps/electron/src/main/network-proxy.ts`), and per-request `proxy`/`tls` in `nessy-server` (`packages/shared/src/config/network-runtime.ts`). `isDeniedNetworkTarget` is NOT applied: it denies 10/8, and the host resolves to `10.219.171.192`.
- **Interfaces:** `ArtifactRecord { id; url; title; expiresAt: string | null; retentionDays: number }`; `ArtifactClientError.kind: 'needs-login' | 'dp-unavailable' | 'forbidden' | 'policy' | 'gone' | 'too-large' | 'invalid' | 'network' | 'server'`.
- **Tests:**
  - Positive:
    - the POST body carries exactly the `publishRequest` fields;
    - an update is a PUT to the same id and keeps the URL;
    - a full document is normalised once (one `<html>` in the stored body);
    - a title containing `</title><script>` is stored escaped.
  - Negative:
    - `printToken` answering `undefined` sends no request and answers `needs-login`; each `printToken` throw maps to its kind;
    - a 401 answers `needs-login` with no second request and no child process;
    - a 302 to another origin answers `network` with no second request;
    - a `url` on another origin is refused before any network call;
    - `T_ARTIFACTS_ORIGIN=…` and `ARTIFACT_AUTH_TOKEN=…` in the environment change nothing;
    - `.pdf`, 16 MiB + 1 and `<input type=password>` are refused locally;
    - an update without `retention_days` leaves `expiresAt` unchanged in the registry;
    - with `BUN_CONFIG_VERBOSE_FETCH` set, no request is sent;
    - the token value appears in no error, log line or result.
- **Done means:** no npx, Node or skill dependency; exactly one fixed network target; the token-custody sentence is on the docs page (UA1.21).

**UA1.17 `[host]` `[safety]` Publish guard: read floor, single-read buffer, secret and PII scan (S–M).**
- **Files:** create `packages/shared/src/artifacts/publish-guard.ts` + test.
  - **Read floor:** the `realpath` must sit inside the session cwd, an accepted added root or the session directory (`isPathWithinDirectory`, `packages/session-tools-core/src/runtime/path-security.ts:22`); anything else is refused before it is read.
  - **Always refused:** every pattern of `SAFE_MODE_CONFIG.deniedPaths` (`mode-types.ts:623`), matched against the realpath through the same `getDeniedPathViolation` matcher; plus `**/.env`, `**/.env.*` and the artifacts registry. No hand-listed paths: a test asserts the list is derived from that constant.
  - **Single read:** the file is read ONCE into a buffer; normalise, scan, preview and send all operate on that buffer, and the guard returns its sha256.
  - **Scan:** `scanToolResultForSecrets` (`packages/shared/src/agent/tool-result-secret-scan.ts:169`) plus blocking rules LOCAL to this guard:
    - `jwt` `/\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}/`;
    - `ory-token` `/\bory_(?:at|rt|st|ht)_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}/`;
    - `bearer` `/\bBearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+\/-]{20,}=*/i`;
    - `pan`: 13–19 digits with optional space/dash separators, Luhn-valid.
    - Non-blocking counts shown on the card: e-mail addresses and phone numbers.
    - UA1.6's key-name scan once it lands.
    - The rules stay local on purpose: widening `TOOL_RESULT_SECRET_RULES` changes every tool result on every runtime, and F1.331 owns that separately.
  - **Card sentence is fixed.** Never "0 findings"; instead: "Automatic check found no known token formats; it does not detect encoded or free-form secrets or client data."
- **Tests:**
  - Positive: a clean file under the cwd passes and returns its digest; a non-Luhn 16-digit number passes.
  - Negative:
    - a cwd symlink to `credentials.enc` is refused, and so is one to `~/.nessy/dp_auth_creds.json.bak`;
    - `~/.npmrc`, `~/.aws/credentials` and a `.env` in the cwd are refused;
    - files carrying a JWT, an `ory_at_…` token or a Luhn-valid PAN are refused, and the refusal names the rule and the line but never the value;
    - a path outside the roots is refused before it is read.
- **Done means:** every refusal is a sentence the model can act on.

**UA1.18 `[host]` `[pi]` `[claude]` `[codex]` `publish_artifact` and `list_artifacts` session tools with the host card (L; needs UA1.16, UA1.17; OQ-22 (a)(b)(c)).**
- **Precedent:** `archive_session` (P6.466): a registry tool whose card lives in the host closure (`SessionManager.ts` `archiveSessionForAgent`, `:7220`; `host_gate`, `noAlwaysAllow`, `countHostRaisedCard`, `releaseOnStop` at `:7246-7268`), plus a codex bridge route (`sessionArchive`, `host-callback-bridge.ts:176`).
- **Files:**
  - `tool-defs.ts`:
    - `publish_artifact`: `executionMode: 'registry'`, `safeMode: 'block'` (so explore and plan block it before any card);
    - `list_artifacts`: `safeMode: 'allow'`, `readOnly: true`;
    - both go in `SESSION_TOOLS_REQUIRING_HOST_CALLBACK` and `SESSION_TOOLS_BRIDGED_BY_HOST_CALLBACK`;
    - the description says: call only when the user asked in this conversation to publish or share a page, otherwise offer it in text, never proactively; it states the access model, the expiry and the 1..30 retention range, and names no npx command;
  - handlers `publish-artifact.ts` and `list-artifacts.ts` with no approval logic (as in `archive-session.ts`); ports in `context.ts`;
  - `SessionManager.ts` `publishArtifactForAgent`: unattended predicate → guard (UA1.17) → normalise → `permission_request` with `origin: 'host_gate'` and `noAlwaysAllow: true` (plus `countHostRaisedCard` and `releaseOnStop`) → re-check the file digest → client → registry → host-written session event;
  - **unattended predicate**, defined ONCE here or in UA6.2 (whichever lands first) and shared with UA6.2's `delete_page`. Refused without a card when:
    - no interactive client is attached;
    - the turn was dispatched by an automation, a schedule or a goal continuation;
    - the caller is a background subagent or a Workflow child;
    - the mode is `dontAsk`.
    - A card that times out resolves to deny. `bypassPermissions` still raises the card;
  - at most one pending publish card per session; a second call while one is pending is refused with a sentence;
  - `retention_days` from the model must be 1..30 (default 7) and is refused outside that range with a sentence; unlimited and longer retention only from the UI (UA1.19);
  - `url` (the update target) is accepted only when its id is in this workspace's registry; any other id is refused ("publish a new page instead");
  - `list_artifacts` returns only registry entries whose `sourceSessionId` is the calling session (id, title, url, expiresAt); the full `/mine` list is UI-only;
  - **audit:** every create, update and delete, and every refusal, appends a host-written session event (never model-writable) with runtime, permission mode, sourcePath, sha256, byte size, scan counts, script count, external hosts, retentionDays, decision (approved / denied / refused-unattended / refused-guard) and timestamp; no content and no token;
  - `session-scoped-tool-callback-registry.ts`, `session-self-management-bindings.ts`, `mergeSessionScopedToolCallbacks`;
  - codex: `HOST_CALLBACK_BRIDGE_ROUTES` (`artifactPublish`, `artifactList`), `backend/codex/session-callback-bridge.ts`, `host-callback-bridge-client.ts`, `session-mcp-server/src/index.ts`;
  - Pi: pin rows in `packages/pi-agent-server/src/__tests__/harness-contract/fixtures/tool-surface.json`;
  - renderer: the host-gate variant of `PermissionRequest.tsx` shows:
    - the access line, the owner and the absolute expiry date;
    - file, size and sha256 prefix;
    - a preview: rendered Markdown, or the HTML in a sandboxed frame with scripts disabled, plus the first 40 source lines;
    - the script count and the external host list;
    - the scan sentence;
    - for an update, a size/line diff against the registry's last digest;
  - `turn-preview.ts` titles; copy in `en.json` and `ru.json`.
- **Card rules:**
  - create: a card every time, wherever the tool is reachable, bypass included;
  - update: a card every time in v1 (new preview plus diff). A per-artifact "update without asking" switch may come later in the UI only; it is never implied by `bypassPermissions` and never reachable over the codex bridge;
  - unattended: refusal and zero requests.
- **Interfaces:**
  - `publish_artifact { file_path; title?; description?; emoji?; url?; retention_days? }` → `{ url, expires_at, updated }`;
  - `list_artifacts {}` → the calling session's entries.
- **Tests:**
  - Positive:
    - Pi, Claude and codex (through the bridge) each reach the one closure and raise the same card;
    - approve → exactly one POST carrying the previewed buffer;
    - an approved update → one PUT.
  - Negative:
    - deny → no request, and a refusal to the model;
    - `bypassPermissions` raises the card on both create and update;
    - each unattended path → refusal before `countHostRaisedCard`, and zero requests;
    - a timed-out card → deny;
    - a file rewritten after the card was raised → nothing sent, and a new card;
    - `retention_days: 0` from the model → refusal, no request;
    - an id from `/mine` that is not in the registry → refusal;
    - `list_artifacts` hides other sessions' artifacts;
    - a second pending call → refused;
    - codex without the bridge → both tools absent;
    - the card offers no "always allow".
- **Done means:** the card is identical on all three runtimes; the product has no Bash, npx or dp-login path; one audit event per decision.

---

**UA1.19 `[renderer]` `[wire]` Artifact chip and the Published pages list (M; needs UA1.18).**
- **Files:**
  - the chip: open, copy, absolute expiry date;
  - the list: `listOwnedArtifacts` joined to the registry;
  - actions:
    - open and copy;
    - extend (`PATCH retention_days`, unlimited included, with its own confirmation);
    - delete: the confirmation reads "Deleting closes the link for everyone. Copies readers already saved, and any service backups, are not recalled.", then `DELETE`;
  - a failed DELETE (`needs-login` or `network`) is kept as a pending deletion in the registry, shown in the list and retried after the next successful sign-in; it is never dropped silently;
  - RPC `artifacts:list`, `artifacts:setRetention` and `artifacts:delete` in `protocol/{dto,channels,routing}.ts` and `channel-map.ts`;
  - i18n in en and ru;
  - playground states: none, live, expiring in under 24 h, expired, needs-login, pending deletion.
- **Tests:**
  - Positive:
    - artifacts published outside Nessy are listed;
    - delete sends one DELETE after the confirmation;
    - a pending deletion is retried after sign-in.
  - Negative:
    - an expired row offers no extend;
    - needs-login shows the single card and opens no browser;
    - a failed DELETE is not dropped.
- **Done means:** a playground sweep of the chip and the list.

---

**UA1.20 `[claude]` `Artifact` alias to the host tool (XS; needs F1.253 or UA1.5, and UA1.18).**
- **Files:**

  - the `publish_artifact` schema also accepts the native `action: 'publish'` shape, per the vendored `sdk-tools.d.ts` `ArtifactInput` (SDK `2.1.251`, `favicon` at `:3072`):
    - it maps `file_path`, `title`, `description`, `url`, and `favicon` → `emoji`;
    - it ignores `label`, `note`, `capabilities`, `contract` and `force`;
  - a `url` whose origin is not the t-artifacts constant is refused;
  - every other native action is refused with a sentence pointing at `list_artifacts`, and a native `delete` never maps onto DELETE;
  - the alias goes through the same card.

  - Negative: `action: 'read'` and `action: 'delete'` are refused; a `url` on `claude.ai` is refused; there is no alias when the `session` server is absent.

**UA1.21 `[docs]` `[prompt]` Guidance in our words and the stated residues (S; OQ-22 (d)).**
- **Files:**
  - the `publish_artifact` description, citing t-artifacts `skills/artifact-publish/skilldata/core.md` at `14e402e` (paraphrase only; licence UNVERIFIED);
  - `apps/electron/resources/docs/artifacts.md`;

- **Stated on the docs page and in the tool's design note:**
  1. The host card is a consent control for the sanctioned path, not an egress control. Any Bash-capable agent with network can reach the service directly, and codex cannot be intercepted.
  2. Token custody (UA1.16).
  3. The link is the only access control; the source is downloadable by id; a deletion does not recall copies.
  4. Page scripts run in the readers' browsers.
- **OQ-22 (d) default "redirect":** on Pi and Claude, a pre-tool-use rule denies Bash commands containing `t-artifacts.t-tech.team`, `artifact-publish` or `@loyalty-personalization/artifact-publish`, with a pointer to `publish_artifact`. On codex this is a stated residue.
- **Not shipped:** the `artifact-publish` skill itself.
- **Tests:**
  - the description names the access model, the expiry and "only when asked", and names no npx command;
  - under redirect, `curl https://t-artifacts.t-tech.team/api/artifacts` on Pi and Claude is denied with the pointer, while `curl https://example.com` is not.

**UA1.22 `[live]` `[measure]` Consented publish sitting (S; owner action).**

**UA1.23 `[renderer]` Publish from an inline preview (S; needs UA1.18, UA1.19; owner 2026-09-23: «Да» — an extension beyond craft, which has no publish on previews).**
- **Files:** the header of `packages/ui/src/components/markdown/MarkdownHtmlBlock.tsx` (beside fullscreen) and of the markdown-preview block gain a "Publish" action; renderer RPC `artifacts:publishFile { sessionId, filePath }` in `protocol/{dto,channels,routing}.ts` + `channel-map.ts`, handled by the SAME host path as `publish_artifact` (UA1.17 guard → UA1.18 card → client → registry → audit event); i18n en and ru; playground knob on the preview entries.
- **Rules:** only a block whose `src` names a file offers the action (inline content without a file does not); the renderer sends the PATH, never content bytes; the card, the retention default (7 days) and every refusal are UA1.18's; the source session is the block's session.
- **Tests:** Positive: Publish on an html-preview with a `src` inside the cwd raises the host card for that file, and approve sends one POST. Negative: no action when `src` is absent; a `src` outside the roots is refused by the guard before any card; an unattended session cannot be reached from this path (a user click is the trigger, but the card still appears in bypass).
- **Done means:** one publishing path in the host for the tool, the Page button (UA6.7) and the preview button.

**UA1.24 `[host]` `[safety]` Shareable chat snapshot: the transcript as it is, minus paths and secrets (M; needs UA1.17; OQ-22 (e), owner 2026-09-23).**
- **Owner's rule:** «нужно отправлять как есть по возможности, то есть не только текст сообщений но и тулы и прочее, но без путей и секретов». Thinking is included as in the chat; paths become relative; the target is t-artifacts.
- **Files:** create `packages/server-core/src/sessions/share-snapshot.ts` + test, built on the existing Markdown export (`formatSessionTranscriptMarkdown` / `formatTranscriptMessage` in `SessionManager.ts`, reused, not forked — move them to this module if that is the smallest honest change):
  - **content kept as in the chat:** user and assistant messages; tool calls with name, status, intent, input and the DISPLAYED result (`toolResult`; `rawToolResult` is a duplicate of it and is not emitted); plans, errors, warnings and info rows; attachments by name;
  - **thinking** (`isThinking` messages) is emitted inside a collapsed `<details><summary>Thinking</summary>` block, and so is every tool call's input and result (the chat shows them collapsed too); whether the service renders `<details>` is measured in UA1.27, and a plain heading is the fallback;
  - **paths:** an absolute path inside the session's working directory or an accepted root becomes relative to it (`src/app.ts`); any other absolute path, `~/…` form or home-directory prefix becomes `…/<basename>`; the header's `Working directory` line is replaced by the project's folder NAME; a test pins that the user's home path and account name never appear;
  - **secrets are MASKED, not refused** (a chat routinely contains them): `scanToolResultForSecrets` plus UA1.17's local rules (JWT, `ory_*`, `Bearer`, Luhn-valid PAN) over every emitted string, each value replaced by a marker naming its rule; counts go to the card;
  - **size:** the whole snapshot must fit 16 MiB; a single tool result larger than the cap chosen in this row is truncated with a visible marker stating the dropped size (the cap is written into the module with its reason); over the service cap the share is refused with a sentence;
  - header keeps title, model, connection name, status and token usage; it drops session id, workspace path and permission-mode internals nobody reading needs (the permission mode itself stays: it explains why tools ran).
- **Tests:** Positive: a fixture session with a Bash call, a Read of a file under the cwd, a thinking block and an error renders all four, with `src/…` relative paths and the thinking inside `<details>`. Negative: an absolute path outside the roots becomes `…/name`; the home directory and the OS user name appear nowhere; a JWT, an `ory_at_…` token, a `Bearer` header and a Luhn-valid PAN in a tool result are masked and counted; `rawToolResult` content that differs from `toolResult` is not emitted; a 20 MiB transcript is refused with a sentence.

**UA1.25 `[host]` `[wire]` Session sharing rewired onto t-artifacts (M; needs UA1.16, UA1.18, UA1.24).**
- **Files:** `SessionManager.ts` `shareToViewer`, `updateShare` and `revokeShare` keep their names, events and the `sharedUrl`/`sharedId` fields, but their bodies change: the raw `StoredSession` JSON POST to `${VIEWER_URL}/s/api` is REMOVED (it is the privacy hole the research names); create = the UA1.24 snapshot through the UA1.16 client (`content_type: 'markdown'`), update = `PUT` to the same id, revoke = `DELETE`; `requireViewerUrl` and the `VIEWER_URL` gate in `packages/shared/src/branding.ts` go; the registry (UA1.16) records the entry with kind `chat`; the session-delete revoke path becomes a question (OQ-22 (g)) and a failed DELETE becomes a pending deletion (UA1.19).
- **Card:** the same host card as UA1.18 on every share and every update (owner: a card on every create and update), showing the access line, the 7-day expiry, the size, the masked-secret and relativised-path counts and a rendered preview of exactly the bytes to be sent. The trigger is ONLY the user's Share action — no agent tool shares a chat.
- **Tests:** Positive: share → one POST of the snapshot bytes shown on the card; update → one PUT to the same id, URL unchanged; stop sharing → one DELETE and the fields cleared. Negative: no request before approval; the POST body never contains a `StoredSession` field name (`rawToolResult`, `storedPath`, `workingDirectory`); a dp needs-login shows the single card and opens no browser; an unattended context cannot reach this path.

**UA1.26 `[renderer]` The Share menu works (S; needs UA1.25).**
- **Files:** `SessionMenu.tsx`, `CompactSessionMenu.tsx` and `ChatPage.tsx` (`RENDERER_CONFIG.sharingEnabled` becomes true and the existing Share → Shared submenu — open, copy link, update, stop sharing — is kept as it is); the success toast shows the link and the expiry; i18n en and ru; the Published pages list (UA1.19) shows chats with a chat badge.
- **Supersedes** the 2026-09-23 note that the refusing Share item is intended: that was the state while sharing had no target.
- **Tests:** Positive: Share → card → Shared submenu with the link. Negative: a declined card leaves the item as Share and sends nothing.

**UA1.27 `[live]` Chat-sharing sitting (S; owner action, with UA1.22).**
- **Proves** on the packaged build, for a Pi, a Claude and a codex session each carrying a tool call, a thinking block, an absolute path and a planted FAKE token: the published page shows the tools and the thinking collapsed, relative paths, the fake token masked; update keeps the URL; stop sharing deletes it; whether the service renders `<details>` (else the fallback). Every id created is deleted in the sitting.

- **Files:**
  - copy+adapt `server-core/src/tasks/TaskRunner.ts` + `TaskRunner.test.ts`;
  - create `tasks/conductor-host.ts`;
  - `SessionManager.ts`: `session_created` for children; one runner per workspace.
- **`SessionConductorHost` behaviour:**
  - `sendMessage(…, { midStreamOverride: 'queue', onAck })`, raced against settlement over the three arms;
  - `getSessionProjectHandleId` replaces `getSessionWorkingDirectory`;
  - completion is accepted only when the session is settled, has no active goal, and has no live monitor, cron or backgrounded record;
  - `node-woke-after-done`;
  - the verdict turn must start after the verify message.
- **Adaptations:**
  - the child's handle and `projectId` are the orchestrator's (never `spec.cwd` or `spec.project`);
  - `spec.cwd` is re-validated per run and resume;
  - `terminalIsError` → a failed node;
  - the running status is written only if configured;
  - an interrupt pauses the run;
  - `assertNodesHostable` before the first dispatch;
  - a completed run closes the orchestrator as upstream does (spec §8, OQ-6).
- **Plugins and their MCP servers in node sessions (owner 2026-09-24: «да, добавь про плагины в UA4.1»).** Upstream has no plugin concept; ours reaches every runtime through ONE host mechanism — the capability snapshot (`packages/shared/src/plugins/session-scoped.ts` states it: the snapshot is where every runtime's commands, skills, agents, hooks and MCP servers come from; `claude-agent.ts` passes the SDK `plugins: []` on purpose). So a node is an ordinary session and gets plugins the ordinary way, with these rules written down:
  - **Enablement follows the orchestrator's scope.** A node session sees exactly the plugins enabled for the workspace and for the orchestrator's project (`PluginEnablementScope` `'workspace' | 'project'`, `hooks/usePluginEnablement.ts`), because the node carries the orchestrator's `projectHandleId`. The spec cannot enable a plugin, and neither can an agent through `create_board_task`.
  - **Session-scoped plugins do NOT pass to nodes.** A bundle the user loaded for the orchestrator session only (D1.69, `session-scoped.ts`) lives for that one session by definition. The editor help says so in one line (i18n en and ru).
  - **Plugin MCP servers, skills, agents and hooks** behave in a node as in any session of that project on that runtime: the same wire names (`pluginMcpWireName`), the same activation grants, the same managed hook policy, the same MCP connection-isolation posture. Each node leases its own MCP connections the way any session does; UA4.12's fd count on four codex nodes covers the cost.
  - **Premise to measure at dispatch — do node `sources` narrow plugin MCP servers?** A grep on 2026-09-24 found no coupling between `enabledSourceSlugs` and the plugin MCP projection, so the expected reading is: `sources` narrows the WORKSPACE sources only, and plugin-provided MCP servers follow plugin enablement. Whichever it is, the editor's sources picker states it, and the run confirmation (UA4.4) lists, per node, the sources AND the active plugins with their MCP server names.
  - **HONEST residue by runtime:** a plugin component a runtime cannot host (the existing per-runtime capability rows, e.g. a hook event codex lacks) is absent in that node exactly as it is in a normal session on that runtime; the run confirmation shows the node's runtime next to its plugins, and nothing is emulated.
- **Tests:**
  - The 31 upstream cases, with the `spec.cwd`-fallback case inverted.
  - `conductor-plugins.test.ts`, on pi, claude and codex fixtures:
    - Positive: a plugin enabled for the workspace exposes its MCP tool and its skill in the node's capability snapshot; a plugin enabled for the orchestrator's project does too.
    - Negative: a plugin enabled only for ANOTHER project is absent; the orchestrator's session-scoped plugin is absent; a `task.yaml` or a `create_board_task` call naming a plugin enables nothing; the snapshot of a node equals the snapshot of a normal new session in the same project on the same runtime (the parity pin).
  - `conductor-host.test.ts`:
    - `max_parallel: 2` dispatches both before either completes;
    - an error after the ack gives exactly one `node-finished` or `node-retry`;
    - a send resolved without an ack fails the node "not delivered";
    - an interrupted child pauses and Resume re-dispatches it;
    - no `createSession` carries `workingDirectory`;
    - `spec.project` other than the orchestrator's → the child gets the orchestrator's;
    - an unsettled idle door defers completion.
  - `conductor-runtimes.test.ts`:
    - a three-node DAG on pi, claude and codex fixtures passes `${nodes.a.output}`;
    - a nestor-cloud node is refused before dispatch;
    - per-runtime `tokenUsage` fixtures.

**UA4.2 `[safety]` `[product]` Child posture (S; OQ-7).**

  - `SessionManager.clampChildPermissionModeToParent`: the Conductor `parentSessionId` becomes a ceiling key;

  - Negative:

    - lowering the orchestrator to `explore` mid-run caps the node's next mode change;
    - a managed-disabled bypass never yields a bypass child;
    - the test fails if `AUTONOMOUS_DEFAULT_MODE` returns.

**UA6.6 `[safety]` Craft share-service guard (XS).**
- **Files:** a test, plus a line in `docs-site/content/go-further/sharing.md` and its ru mirror saying that Pages cannot be published yet.
- **What it forbids:** Craft's share service, not sharing:
  - the Craft host (`thecraftagents`);
  - the upstream share-API default;
  - the Craft share-service knobs: `CRAFT_PAGES_SHARE_API_URL`, `CRAFT_FEATURE_PAGES_SHARING`, and their `NESSY_` rebrands.

  It names no share channel, key or field, because publishing is DEFERRED (UA6.7), not forbidden.
- **Tests:** `sharing-cut.test.ts` (the file name is kept).
  - Positive control: the same scan over a temporary file carrying the upstream default share-API URL reports it. Every needle (the host, the URL and each knob name) is assembled at runtime, so the test file never matches itself.
  - Negative: `git grep -a thecraftagents` over `packages apps` answers 0, and so does each knob name.

**UA6.7 `[product]` Pages publishing through t-artifacts (M–L; OQ-18 resolved 2026-09-23 by the owner: «по артефактам да, использовать то что выше дал»; needs UA1.16–UA1.18 and the 6+7 unit).**
- **Target:** the host publisher. No Craft code, no password, no stored credential: the owner is the user's dp identity.
- **Transform:** one self-contained page. The snapshot is inlined as a JSON `<script>`, and a read-only shim answers `nessy-pages/v1` reads from it; writes and actions answer "view-only".
- **Port (re-derived):**
  - `share-bundle.ts`: the field allowlist, refusal on a script grant, the view-only ack and the parse check; caps re-derived against the single 16 MiB file;
  - `SharePageDialog.tsx`;
  - channels `pages:getShareCapabilities`, `pages:getShareDataScan`, `pages:publish`, and `pages:unpublish` (a DELETE);
  - `pages:setPublicationPassword` is DROPPED, because the service has no passwords;
  - publication types `{ artifactId, url, expiresAt, publishedAt, contentDigest }`.
- **Guard and card:** the UA1.17 guard runs over both the HTML and the snapshot; the card is the same as UA1.18's.
- **Tests:**
  - a page with a script grant is refused;
  - the scan runs over both the HTML and the snapshot;
  - unpublish sends one DELETE.
- **Done means:** the live case PASSES; the row lands after the 6+7 unit.

## Phase 8: Script action and refresh (`UA8.1`–`UA8.5`)

Pages lane: a unit of its own, merged after 6+7.

**UA8.1 `[host]` `[safety]` Script action (L; OQ-4, decided: a general automation action, available, digest-approved).**
- **Files:**
  - copy+adapt `automations/script-executor.ts` + test. `runApprovedScript` is the only export. It reads the script bytes once, verifies their sha256 against the ledger, writes them to a private temporary file in a 0700 directory, and spawns the interpreter on that copy with upstream's working directory. Stated residue: only the entry file is pinned, so an approved script is a single self-contained file;
  - copy+adapt `handlers/script-handler.ts`;
  - `automations/utils.ts` env builder: upstream platform essentials plus the named keys, with NO prefix loop;
  - `types.ts` (`ScriptAction` into our union), `schemas.ts`, `validation.ts`, `index.ts`, `name-utils.ts`, `handlers/index.ts`, `automation-system.ts`;
  - the injected audit port, wired by `SessionManager.ts` to `privilegedExecutionBroker.auditEvent`;
  - ledger approvals;
  - `isLocalAutomationManagedDisabled` + `describeManagedLocalAutomationRefusal`, checked at dispatch. This is the existing managed policy, applied as to every local automation; there is no new gate;
  - the `rpc/automations.ts` Test hunk through the wrapper.
- **Tests:**
  - Positive: an approved, contained script runs; `NESSY_PAGE_DATA_DIR` is present for a page.
  - Negative:
    - a symlink escape is blocked;
    - a changed digest does not run, from Test either;
    - a script replaced between the hash and the spawn (an injected hook) runs the approved bytes or refuses, and never runs the replacement;
    - a timeout ends in SIGKILL;
    - with `NESSY_DP_AUTH_TOKEN`, `NESSY_CLI_DP_AUTH_TOKEN`, `NESSY_SERVER_TOKEN` and `NESSY_WH_API_TOKEN` set in the parent, none reaches a spawned child;
    - a guard finds no raw-executor importer;
    - managed-disabled refuses, including a policy that turns on after the `AutomationSystem` was built.
  - Boundary: with managed `disableAllHooks` alone, the test asserts and documents, for each trigger kind, which path (host `AutomationSystem` or per-session runtime hooks) runs a script action and what happens to it, whichever it is.

**UA8.2 `[host]` `[safety]` Scheduled refresh (M).**
- **Files:**
  - copy `pages/refresh.ts`;
  - `automation-system.ts` (`reloadPageRefreshMatchers`): a matcher exists only for an approved refresh;
  - the watcher;
  - `pages/storage.ts`: the host writes `refresh.<ext>` from content; the digest; `lastRefresh`;
  - `tool-defs.ts`: `PageRefreshSpecInputSchema` RW, content not path. This removes UA6.3's withheld `refresh` range;
  - `SessionManager.ts`.
- **Managed policy:** the existing managed-local-automation policy applies through all three of its arms:
  - the `enableScheduler` construction gate;
  - the `subscribeManagedLocalAutomationChange` enforcement;
  - a use-time `isLocalAutomationManagedDisabled()` check at refresh dispatch (the D1.409 shape).

  The construction gate alone cannot stop a system built before the codex tier is written.
- **Tests:** `refresh-consent.test.ts`.
  - Positive: an approved refresh has a `page:<slug>` matcher.
  - Negative:
    - a tool-set spec stays inert;
    - a script edit disables it;
    - with local automation managed-disabled, no refresh runs;
    - the policy turning on after the `AutomationSystem` was built stops the next refresh;
    - an agent-supplied path is rejected by the schema.

**UA8.3 `[renderer]` `[wire]` Script and refresh surfaces (S).**
- **Files:**
  - `rpc/automations.ts`: the hunk, plus `automations:approveScript`, which records `{ path, sha256, args, runtime }` in the ledger;
  - `rpc/pages.ts`: `pages:approveRefresh` and `pages:runRefreshNow`. These are ours; upstream has no approval or run-now channel;
  - `channels.ts`, `routing.ts`, `channel-map.ts`, `shared/types.ts`, `ipc-channels.test.ts`, and the ipc-inventory regeneration;
  - the automation action row (approve script);
  - `PageView.tsx`: refresh status, approve and run now, removing UA7.3's refresh range;
  - their keys.
- **Tests:** `script-action-row.test.tsx`. Positive: a result renders; approving records a ledger entry. Negative: a blocked run shows its reason; a foreign workspace is refused on each new channel.

**UA8.4 `[docs]` Refresh and script docs (S).**
- **Files:** `pages.md` (refresh); `automations.md` (script action, approval, the single-file residue, the network residue).

**UA8.5 `[live]` Refresh and script-action sitting case (S).**
- **Proves (refresh):**
  - the approval is asked once;
  - a cron run updates the page and writes an audit line;
  - a script edit re-asks.
- **Proves (script action):**
  - a user-authored `type:'script'` automation with no approval is refused, and the refusal shows in history;
  - after approval on the automation row, it runs;
  - editing the script refuses the next run;
  - a parent `NESSY_DP_AUTH_TOKEN` is absent from the child's env;
  - the Test button follows the same rule.

## Phase 11: Close-out 1.42.0 (`UA11.1`–`UA11.7`)

**UA11.1 `[close-out]` `[product]` Release-cut audit and deferral (S).**
- **Audit.** For each phase on the release branch and each merged pages unit, record two things:
  - it is user-complete: no visible entry leads to a withheld range or a missing handler;
  - every live case of its capabilities PASSED in the packaged sitting.

  The cut falls only at a valid boundary (Sequencing). A finding UA1.10 or UA7.6 routes here as blocking holds its unit out until its row lands.
- **Cross-lane pairs.** If Projects and the Pages 6+7 unit both ship:
  - the project DELETE cascade reaches pages (UA5.3, UA6.2);
  - the `PagesHome` and `PageView` project ranges are removed (UA5.9, UA7.3), and `PageView` offers move-to-project.

  A gap becomes a row before the cut.
- **Deferral.** A pages unit not merged by the cut, or a board phase after the named boundary:
  - stays out of 1.42.0; a pages unit stays on `feat/TCODE-000-1-42-0-pages`;
  - has its rows close as `moved → <successor row>` in the 1.43.0 plan, with Commit `—`. The 1.43.0 plan cites their shas only after that unit merges there;
  - keeps its keys, docs, docs-site pages, capability-ledger rows and architecture record out of 1.42.0.

  **UA6.7:** if the owner's resource has not arrived, or UA6.7 is not complete with its live case PASSING, it closes as `moved → <successor row>` in the 1.43.0 plan, keeping OQ-18 open there.
- **Files:** the successor rows in the 1.43.0 plan; the KNOWN list handed to UA11.3. The controller pins this plan's cells.
- **Done means:** every shipped capability has a PASS row in `docs/testing/cdp-live-checklist.md`, and nothing incomplete ships.

**UA11.2 `[close-out]` Version bump (XS).** 1.42.0 in both `package.json` files. `bun.lock` is not part of it.

**UA11.3 `[close-out]` Release notes (S).** `apps/electron/resources/release-notes/1.42.0.md`, new-file recipe.
- **NEW**, each listed only if its phase or unit shipped (UA11.1). All are available features:
  - the board, tasks, planning, projects;
  - Pages (library, viewer, six agent tools, thumbnails);
  - scheduled page refresh;
  - page source actions with grants;
  - the automation script action.
- **IMPROVED:** codex status and labels.
- **FIXED:** the status example.
- **KNOWN:**
  - no keyboard drag;
  - the script network residue and the single-file residue;
  - background work vs node completion;
  - the OQ-6 and OQ-7 posture changes, including the Conductor's close of a completed run;
  - if Pages shipped, that pages cannot be published or shared yet.

**UA11.4 `[close-out]` docs-site final sync (S).** en and ru.

**UA11.5 `[close-out]` CDP checklist and packaged sitting (M).**
- One row per UA case, with narratives in `docs/testing/cdp-runs/`, the `env -u` launcher and dark theme.
- Every case of every shipped capability PASSES before the cut. A failing case holds its phase or unit out (UA11.1).

**UA11.6 `[close-out]` Regenerate (XS).** The controller regenerates the parity report and the deviation register from a clean tree.

**UA11.7 `[close-out]` Final gate (M).**
- Every UA test file run file by file through `bun scripts/bun-test-lock.ts`, with the FILE COUNT read.
- `bun run typecheck:all` and `bun run lint` (detached), and `bun run validate:ci`.
- `python3 scripts/sort-plan-tracker.py --check <this plan>`.
- `CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false bash apps/electron/scripts/build-dmg.sh arm64`.
- `git status` clean.
- Record the real output.

## Port manifest

**How to read it.**
- **Line counts:** `git show v0.13.4:<path> | wc -l`. "hunk" means feature hunks only.
- **Disposition codes:**

  | Code | Meaning |
  | --- | --- |
  | CV | copy-verbatim |
  | CA | copy+adapt |
  | CA, staged | copy+adapt with withheld ranges recorded in the header |
  | RW | rewrite |
  | D(a), D(b), D(c) | drop, with its reason |
  | KEEP | already ours |
  | MEAS | measure, then decide |
  | DEFERRED | awaiting the owner's internal artifact skill/resource (UA6.7) |
- **Target:** "same" means the same relative path. `…/kanban/` means `apps/electron/src/renderer/components/app-shell/kanban/`.

### H. Out of scope

Every other path in `git -C .references/craft-agents-oss diff --name-only v0.10.5 v0.13.4` that is not listed in A–G belongs to a separate adoption review. Examples:
- the whatsapp worker and copilot auth;
- the Pi credential and model files;
- `context-usage*`, `persistent-input.ts`, `pending-steers.ts`, `developer-context.ts`, `workspace-avatar*`, `input-viewport`.

`BackgroundFinishedChip.tsx`, `atoms/background-finished.ts` (UA1.15), `input/input-event-guards.ts` `shouldRecallPromptOnArrowUp` (UA1.13) and `transfer-targets.ts` + test (UA1.14) moved IN on 2026-09-23 by the owner's OQ-20 answers, all copy+adapt.

A host mirror is registered once on UA5.2's setProjectConfigWrittenHook (installProjectContextMirror, called line-neutrally from createStorageRuntime). After every config write it writes projects/<slug>/context.md and records its sha256 as a MACed project-context ledger entry. resolveProjectContextLayer, called by the capability input provider for a turn context carrying projectId (CapabilityContext.projectId by interface merge, set per turn by SessionManager.createCapabilityTurn line for line), binds that file as one instruction record only for bytes the ledger vouches for. Otherwise it logs project-context-tampered, re-renders from config.json (never from the file's bytes), writes the render back and records it; a symlink is not read through, no key gives an unverified render, and a never-mirrored project is rendered as missing. The resolver's instruction arm and toAgentRuntime carry the record to promptLayers unchanged, and BaseAgent's pinned block delivers the same bytes on pi, anthropic and codex-runtime. An edit was measured to reach the next resolve with a new hash and a new capability generation, so no drift notice is ported, and claude-agent.ts, pi-agent.ts, core/types.ts, views.ts and system.ts get no hunk. | UA12.19 | `[safety]` `[CRITICAL]` Share snapshot: a PEM private key written with JSON-escaped newlines (service-account JSON, .env, a Write input) keeps its base64 body while the card counts it masked — treat literal \n / \r\n separators as line breaks in the PEM block rule, and stop a stored marker from swallowing prose up to a later END line | ✅ | `644f26790` | Origin: UA wave 2 UA1.24 recheck (HIGH) (2026-10-02). Control: buildShareSnapshot over a service-account JSON toolResult, the same JSON as a Write toolInput content, and a .env PRIVATE_KEY="-----BEGIN RSA PRIVATE KEY-----\n…\n-----END RSA PRIVATE KEY-----" each emit no base64 body line and count one private-key per block; real-newline blocks unchanged; a marker followed by prose and a later END line keeps the prose. CRITICAL (security: a private key leaves the host in a share while the card says masked); fixed at once by lane UA-cr2. (UA-cr2, 2026-10-02) 644f26790 — share-snapshot.ts's mask-mode PEM rule treats real and JSON-escaped line breaks (\n, \\n, \r\n written out) as key lines and admits \/ in the body, so a service-account JSON (tool result with header or stored marker, or Write input) and a .env PRIVATE_KEY emit no body line and count one private-key per block. After a stored marker the body must be whole base64 or legacy lines, or blank-joined 40+ words (MARKER_TO_END), so prose up to a later END is kept and that END is masked alone. The orphan-END walk is now a char walk over the same breaks. Groups repeat per \/ only, because JavaScriptCore silently returns no match on a per-character group over a megabyte (measured). The to-END rules are skipped when no END follows. The Residue paragraph is updated. Three new arms were red first (3/18), then green 18/18 in 1 file. Four mutants were each killed and restored by sha256. The shared private-key rule and the publish guard are untouched. Verifier: sound/NONE. |

That covers Write, Edit, redirect, cp over it, sed -i, python, rm (with the bare name too), mv away, unlink and `: >`. On codex the name joins CODEX_DATA_FOLDER_CONTROL_RECORDS, so F1.693/F1.717 refuse a patch, an escalated command or a write grant that names it in every mode. Codex residue: with the sandbox master switch off, an un-escalated `rm` of the key runs with no approval, the same as for the global hooks.json and credentials.enc.
